2026-10-28T11:34:13.9577956Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] Run the Decode prologue gate as its own torchrun so the selection can measure the bundle

Serving run 40660502072 (image 40648657907, same configuration as the 9235.28
run plus the in-job Decode prologue selection) scored 9204.30 tok/s (valid,
5805 requests, good fraction 0.9826, acceptance 2.208), but the prologue
selection never measured the candidate: prologue=1. The router cluster check and
the full MegaMoE serving gate with DG_SM90_DECODE_PREPARE=1 passed on all four
ranks; the failure came after them, when select-firmus-decode-prologue.py called
dist.init_process_group a second time on the same torchrun rendezvous after the
gate's own destroy_process_group. Rank 2 failed in NCCL bootstrap with
"socketPollConnect: connect to 198.18.151.54<53561> returned Connection
refused", consistent with reading the previous group's stale unique id from the
reused store key. Serving therefore ran on the control kernels, so 9204.30 is a
control repeat on the new image (-0.34% vs 9235.28, within run-to-run spread).

Fix: select-firmus-decode-kernels.sh now runs check-firmus-megamoe.py
--megamoe-only with DG_SM90_DECODE_PREPARE=1 SGLANG_DECODE_ROUTER=cluster as a
separate torchrun (fresh standalone rendezvous, log prologue-gate.log), and only
on success runs the selection script, which keeps the router check and the
timing but no longer embeds the gate. Timeouts 150 s + 200 s replace the single
260 s; the gate took about 90 s in 40660502072. Decode was ready 7m44s after job
start without profile, so the longer selection fits the 17-minute limit.
